Abstract: We present a training-free approach for controllable 3D inpainting based on initial noise optimization. In the structured 3D latent diffusion framework, we observe that the underlying geometric structure is established during the early stages of the diffusion process and exhibits high sensitivity to the initial noise. Such characteristics compromise stability in tasks like inpainting and editing, where the model must ensure strict alignment with the existing context while synthesizing a new structure. In this paper, we introduce a strategy to optimize the initial noise within the structured 3D latent diffusion framework, ensuring high-fidelity 3D inpainting. Specifically, we update the initial noise by leveraging a backpropagation approximation grounded in the rectified flow model, with the spectral parameterization specially designed for robust and efficient structured 3D latent optimization. Experiments demonstrate consistent improvements in contextual consistency and prompt alignment over representative training-free inpainting baselines, establishing initial noise control as an independent dimension for 3D inpainting, orthogonal to conventional sampling trajectory manipulation.

</details>


### [39] [Prediction of Alzheimer's Disease Risk Factors from Retinal Images via Deep Learning: Development and Validation of Biologically Relevant Morphological Associations in the UK Biobank](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00665)
*Seowung Leem,Yunchao Yang,Adam J. Woods,Ruogu Fang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文利用深度学习模型从彩色眼底照片(CFP)中预测12个与阿尔茨海默病(AD)相关的风险因素（如年龄、性别、吸烟、饮酒等），发现模型表现优于传统视网膜形态学方法，且显著性区域集中于视神经头和视网膜血管。模型生成的显著性评分在AD发病前平均8.55年的病例与对照组间存在显著差异，表明CFP可能编码与AD易感性相关的视网膜结构变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明系统性、代谢性和生活方式因素与AD存在流行病学关联，但尚不清楚彩色眼底照片中是否包含对应这些AD相关风险域的视网膜结构特征。本文旨在探究深度学习能否从CFP中预测AD风险因素，并刻画其背后的视网膜结构基础。

Method: 基于UK Biobank的62,876张CFP图像（来自44,501名参与者），训练深度学习模型预测6个分类变量（性别、吸烟、失眠、经济状况、饮酒、抑郁）和6个连续变量（年龄、教育完成年龄、BMI、收缩压、舒张压、HbA1c）。评估模型性能、显著性图及基于显著性得分的CAM-Score，并与视网膜形态学指标及机器学习模型进行比较。进一步在AD发病前平均8.55年的病例与匹配对照组间比较这些得分。

Result: 深度学习模型性能范围为：分类变量AUROC 0.5654-0.9480，连续变量R² -0.0291-0.7620，优于大多数形态学-机器学习模型。显著性图一致突出视神经头和视网膜血管等生物学意义区域。多个基于显著性的得分在AD前驱病例与对照组间存在显著差异。

Conclusion: CFP编码了与AD风险因素相关的视网膜特征。尽管不具诊断性，深度学习衍生的视网膜表征可能揭示与AD易感性相关的有生物学意义的风险相关结构变化。

Abstract: The systemic, metabolic, lifestyle factors have established associations with Alzheimer's Disease (AD) through epidemiologic and AD-specific biomarker studies. Whether colored fundus photography (CFP) contains retinal structural signatures corresponding to these AD-related risk domains remains unclear. To determine whether deep learning (DL) models can predict 12 AD-related risk factors from CFP and to characterize the retinal structures underlying these predictions, thereby assessing whether CFP reflects pathways to AD vulnerability. Using UK Biobank CFPs, DL models were trained using 62,876 images from 44,501 unique participants to predict 12 factors linked to AD incidence: 6 categorical (sex, smoking, sleeplessness, economic status, alcohol use, depression) and 6 continuous (age, age at completing education, BMI, systolic, diastolic blood pressure, HbA1c). Model performance, model saliency, and saliency-derived scores (CAM-Score) were evaluated and compared to retinal morphometry. The scores were also compared between incident-AD cases (average 8.55 years before onset) and matched controls. Performance of DL ranged from AUROC= 0.5654-0.9480 for categorical and R2=-0.0291-0.7620 for continuous factors, outperforming most of the morphometry-machine learning models. Saliency-based score consistently highlighted biologically meaningful regions, particularly the optic nerve head and retinal vasculature. It also aligned with present morphometric variations. Several saliency-based scores differed significantly between incident AD and matched controls, suggesting potential overlap between retinal correlates of risk factors and preclinical AD-associated changes. CFP encodes retinal signatures linked to AD risk factors. Although not diagnostic, DL-derived retinal representations may uncover biologically meaningful risk-related structural changes mirroring the potential AD vulnerability.

</details>


### [40] [DMDSC: A Dynamic-Margin Deep Simplex Classifier for Open-Set Recognition on Medical Image Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00675)
*Vishal,Arnav Aditya,Nitin Kumar,Saurabh J. Shigwan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对医学影像数据集中的极端类别不平衡问题，本文提出DMDSC框架，通过动态边界机制自动调整类别特定边界，对罕见病理施加更高惩罚和更紧密的特征聚类，在多个医学基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像数据集存在严重的类别不平衡（罕见病理样本极少），这对开放集识别（OSR）构成双重挑战：既要保持已知类别的高分类精度，又要可靠拒斥未知样本。现有DSC和UCDSC方法虽然利用Neural Collapse实现最大类间分离，但其使用的统一边界无法适应医学类别密度的差异。

Method: 提出DMDSC框架，引入动态边界（Dynamic Margin）方法，基于标签频率自动调整各类别边界：对稀有病理类别施加更高的惩罚和更紧密的特征聚类，以抵消数据不平衡的影响。

Result: 在BloodMNIST、OCTMNIST、DermaMNIST和BreaKHis四个医学基准数据集上的广泛实验表明，所提框架优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: DMDSC通过动态边界机制有效解决了医学开放集识别中类别不平衡问题，为临床场景下的稀有病理识别提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Medical imaging datasets are often characterized by extreme class imbalances, where rare pathologies are significantly underrepresented compared to common conditions. This imbalance poses a dual challenge for Open-Set Recognition (OSR): models must maintain high classification accuracy on known classes while reliably rejecting unknown samples unseen during training in the clinical settings. While recently proposed Deep Simplex Classifier (DSC)~\cite{cevikalp2024reaching} and UnCertainty-aware Deep Simplex Classifier (UCDSC)~\cite{Aditya_2026_WACV} successfully leverage Neural Collapse to ensure maximal inter-class separation, they rely on a uniform margin that does not account for the varying densities of medical classes.
  In this paper, we propose DMDSC an enhanced framework featuring a dynamic margin approach. Our approach automatically adapts class-specific margins based on label frequency, enforcing a higher penalty and tighter feature clustering for rare pathologies to counteract the effects of data imbalance. Extensive experiments conducted on diverse medical benchmarks on BloodMNIST\cite{medmnistv2}, OCTMNIST\cite{medmnistv2}, DermaMNIST\cite{medmnistv2}, and BreaKHis~\cite{spanhol2015dataset} datasets, demonstrate that our framework outperforms state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [41] [Foundation AI Models for Aerosol Optical Depth Estimation from PACE Satellite Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00678)
*Zahid Hassan Tushar,Sanjay Purushotham*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ViTCG，一种基于Vision Transformer和通道分组空间回归的框架，利用超光谱遥感数据反演气溶胶光学厚度（AOD），相比现有基础模型均方误差降低62%，并生成空间一致的反演结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于物理的AOD反演方法依赖辐射传输模型、查表法和辅助气象数据，计算量大且空间一致性差；现有数据驱动方法未能充分利用超光谱影像的空间-光谱相干性，导致反演结果空间不一致且对噪声敏感。因此，需要一种能联合建模空间上下文与光谱信息的方法。

Method: 提出ViTCG（Vision Transformer with Channel-wise Grouping），以超光谱大气顶辐亮度为输入，通过Vision Transformer联合建模空间上下文和光谱信息，采用通道分组策略进行空间回归，实现端到端的AOD反演。

Result: 基于PACE卫星辐射观测数据的验证表明，相比Prithvi等最先进的基础模型，ViTCG在均方误差上降低62%，并生成空间连续的AOD场。

Conclusion: 首次探索将基础AI模型用于AOD反演，ViTCG通过联合空间-光谱建模显著提升了反演精度和空间一致性，为超光谱遥感中的气溶胶反演提供了新范式。

Abstract: Aerosol Optical Depth (AOD) retrieval is essential for Earth observation, supporting applications from air quality monitoring to climate studies. Conventional physics-based AOD retrieval methods formulate the problem as a pixel-wise inversion, relying on radiative transfer modeling, memory-intensive look-up tables, and auxiliary meteorological data. While recent data-driven approaches have shown promise, many fail to exploit the spatial-spectral coherence of hyperspectral imagery, leading to spatially inconsistent and noise-sensitive retrievals. We present the first study exploring Foundation AI models for AOD retrieval and propose ViTCG, a Vision Transformer with Channel-wise Grouping-based spatial regression framework that reduces retrieval bias and error. ViTCG uses hyperspectral top-of-atmosphere radiance as input and jointly models spatial context and spectral information. Validation with PACE radiance observations demonstrates a 62% reduction in mean squared error compared to state-of-the-art foundation models, including Prithvi, and produces spatially coherent AOD fields.

</details>


### [42] [PhysEdit: Physically-Consistent Region-Aware Image Editing via Adaptive Spatio-Temporal Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00707)
*Guandong Li,Mengxia Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PhysEdit提出空间与时间双自适应推理框架，通过CARD模块动态分配推理步数与推理token长度，以及SRM模块限制推理区域，在不重新训练的情况下提速1.18倍且保持编辑质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理式图像编辑器对所有指令采用固定的推理策略，但不同编辑指令（如颜色替换、物体插入、物理动作编辑）对空间覆盖范围和推理深度需求各异，缺乏自适应性。

Method: 两个推理时模块：(1) CARD根据指令和参考图像预测编辑复杂度，动态分配推理步数N_r和推理token长度r；(2) SRM从交叉注意力中提取指令条件化的空间先验，将推理限制在语义需要的区域。

Result: 在ImgEdit Basic-Edit Suite上，PhysEdit相比强基线获得1.18x加速（64.3s vs 76.1s），指令遵循度略有提升（CLIP-T +0.7%），身份保持基本持平。外观级编辑加速达1.52x。

Conclusion: 将固定的推理调度转变为条件计算问题，验证了自适应推理分配是效率提升的主要来源。

Abstract: Image editing instructions are heterogeneous: a color swap, an object insertion, and a physical-action edit all demand different spatial coverage and different reasoning depth, yet existing reasoning-based editors apply a single fixed inference recipe to every instruction. We argue that adaptivity along both the spatial and temporal axes is the missing degree of freedom, and we present PhysEdit, an editing framework built around this principle. PhysEdit introduces two inference-time modules that compose without retraining the backbone. At its core, (1) Complexity-Adaptive Reasoning Depth (CARD) predicts edit complexity directly from the instruction and reference image and allocates the reasoning step count N_r and reasoning-token length r per sample -- turning a previously fixed inference schedule into a conditional-computation problem. CARD is supported by (2) a Spatial Reasoning Mask (SRM) that extracts an instruction-conditioned spatial prior from cross-attention to confine reasoning to regions that semantically require it. On the full 737-case ImgEdit Basic-Edit Suite, PhysEdit delivers a 1.18x wall-clock speedup (64.3s vs. 76.1s per sample) over a strong reasoning baseline while slightly improving instruction adherence (CLIP-T 0.2283 vs. 0.2266, +0.7%) and matching identity preservation within noise (CLIP-I 0.8246 vs. 0.8280). The speedup is category-dependent and reaches 1.52x on appearance-level edits, validating CARD's adaptive allocation as the principal source of efficiency gain. A 30-sample pilot with full ablations isolates the contribution of each module.

</details>

*Qiancheng Zhou,Wenhua Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GSACP，一种端到端的单点监督红外小目标检测方法，通过批量内点锚定特征亲和传播在线生成点对掩模监督，避免了外部伪标签构建循环。研究发现自参照传播漂移问题，并通过局部EMA教师解耦、硬背景对比分离和自适应支撑几何等策略加以缓解。在SIRST3数据集上，GSACP-Final以0.6674 mIoU实现超低虚警率，虚警伪影相对PAL降低38%。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有单点监督IRSTD方法依赖显式的离线伪标签构建（如多阶段主动学习、物理驱动掩模生成），流程复杂且需要外部标签演化循环。本文探索一种更简洁的替代方案：通过在线批量内特征亲和传播直接生成点对掩模监督，消除外部循环，实现端到端训练。

Method: 提出GSACP框架：1) 基于硬边距特征亲和度（由局部图像先验门控）的在线点对掩模传播机制；2) 识别并形式化自参照传播漂移问题（SRPD），即表示-监督自指涉纠缠导致特征空间扭曲；3) 系统性地通过单变量消融实验隔离失效模式，包括局部EMA教师解耦、硬背景对比分离、自适应支撑几何等策略。

Result: 在SIRST3数据集上，GSACP-Final达到0.6674 mIoU，虚警伪影（Fa）相对PAL降低38%，建立了超低虚警率的运行状态。

Conclusion: 批量内特征传播作为一种紧凑的端到端替代方案，在虚警抑制至关重要的部署场景中具有竞争力。通过系统解构端到端范式，本文绘制了其性能边界。

Abstract: Single-point supervised infrared small target detection (IRSTD) drastically reduces dense annotation costs. Current state-of-the-art (SOTA) methods achieve high precision by recovering mask supervision through explicit, offline pseudo-label construction, such as multi-stage active learning and physics-driven mask generation. In this paper, we study a minimalist alternative: generating point-to-mask supervision online through in-batch, point-anchored feature-affinity propagation. We instantiate this paradigm as GSACP, an end-to-end testbed that directly supervises the detector using hard-margin feature affinity gated by local image priors, entirely eliminating external label-evolution loops.
  This compact design, however, exposes an optimization bottleneck. Because the affinity target is generated from the same feature representation being optimized, training forms a self-referential loop. We theoretically formalize this as \emph{Self-Referential Propagation Drift}, a representation-supervision entanglement that can sharpen true boundaries or distort the feature space to satisfy its own targets. To systematically isolate these failure modes, we apply a protocolized single-variable ablation procedure spanning local EMA teacher decoupling, hard-background contrastive separation, and adaptive support geometry.
  On the SIRST3 dataset, GSACP-Final establishes a new ultra-low false-alarm operating regime, achieving a highly competitive $0.6674$ mIoU while demonstrating a $38\% relative reduction in false-positive artifacts ($\mathrm{Fa}$) compared with PAL. By systematically deconstructing the end-to-end paradigm, we map its performance boundaries and show that in-batch feature propagation provides a compact alternative for deployment scenarios where false-alarm suppression is paramount.

</details>

*Mohammad Aamir Sohail,Gabriela Pinheiro,Yasemin Poyraz Kocak,Batuhan Hangun,Emre Camkerten,Simge Yigit,Hafize Asude Ertan*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将经典的Sobel边缘检测和Harris角点检测算法移植到量子计算框架中，探索量子图像处理在特征提取任务中的可行性与性能表现。

Method: 采用FRQI和QPIE两种量子图像编码方法；提出基于lag-2差分的量子梯度计算方案，可在叠加态中评估梯度特征；对量子电路识别的候选角点施加经典后处理以降低误检率。

Result: 量子电路输出与经典Sobel和Harris算子结果一致；QPIE编码比FRQI产生更稳定、更连贯的结果，尤其在有限测量次数下；梯度计算在电路级别高效，但整体成本仍由状态制备、测量和经典后处理主导。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了经典边缘和角点检测方法的量子实现是功能可行且可扩展的，但并非端到端的加速方案；在无噪声仿真下验证，NISQ硬件上的性能可能受噪声和测量限制影响。

Abstract: Edge detection refers to identifying points in a digital image where intensity changes sharply, indicating object boundaries or structural features. Corners are locations where gray-level intensity changes abruptly in multiple directions and are widely used in feature extraction, object tracking, and 3D modeling. In this study, we present a quantum implementation of Sobel-based edge detection and Harris-style corner detection. Two quantum image encoding methods - Flexible Representation of Quantum Images (FRQI) and Quantum Probability Image Encoding (QPIE) - are used to encode the input data and are comparatively analyzed. The proposed approach introduces a quantum gradient computation scheme based on lag-2 differences, enabling the evaluation of gradient-like features in superposition. To improve detection quality and reduce false positives, a classical post-processing step is applied to candidate corner points identified by the quantum circuit. Results show that the proposed quantum circuits produce outputs consistent with classical Sobel and Harris operators. Furthermore, the QPIE-based configuration yields more stable and coherent results than FRQI, especially under limited measurement shots. While gradient computation can be performed efficiently at the circuit level, the overall cost remains dominated by state preparation, measurement, and classical post-processing. All experiments are conducted under noiseless simulation, and performance on NISQ hardware may be affected by noise and measurement limitations. Therefore, this work demonstrates a functional and scalable quantum realization of classical edge and corner detection methods rather than an end-to-end speedup.

</details>

*Lin Che,Xi Wang,Marc Pollefeys,Konrad Schindler,Martin Raubal,Peter Kiefer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Place Pulse-Gaze数据集（街景图像+眼动追踪+主观感知标签），并构建Gaze-Guided Urban Perception Framework，通过三个实验设置证明注视信息能有效提升主观城市感知的预测性能，强调了在场景理解中融入人类感知过程的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有计算模型直接从街景图像建模城市感知，忽略了人类通过注视形成主观判断的感知过程。

Method: 构建Place Pulse-Gaze数据集（街景图像+同步眼动追踪+个体感知标签），提出Gaze-Guided Urban Perception Framework，包含三个设置：注视单独建模、注视融合显式语义场景表示、注视融合隐式丰富视觉表示。

Result: 注视本身包含有效预测信号；融合注视与场景表示（语义和视觉）均能进一步提升主观城市感知的预测性能。

Conclusion: 将人类感知过程融入城市场景理解具有重要意义，为注视引导的多模态城市计算开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Urban perception describes how people subjectively evaluate urban environments, shaping how cities are experienced and understood. Existing computational approaches primarily model urban perception directly from street view images, but largely ignore the human perceptual process through which such judgments are formed. In this paper, we introduce Place Pulse-Gaze, an urban perception dataset that augments street view images with synchronized eye-tracking recordings and individual perception labels. Based on this dataset, we propose a Gaze-Guided Urban Perception Framework to study how gaze behavior contributes to the modeling of subjective urban perception. The framework systematically investigates three complementary settings: gaze-only modeling, gaze fusion with explicit semantic scene representations, and gaze fusion with implicit richer visual representations. Experiments show that gaze alone already carries useful predictive signals for subjective urban perception, and that integrating gaze with scene representations further improves prediction under both semantic and richer visual representations. Overall, our findings highlight the importance of incorporating human perceptual processes into urban scene understanding and open a direction for gaze-guided multimodal urban computing.

</details>


### [46] [Make Your LVLM KV Cache More Lightweight](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00789)
*Xihao Chen,Yangyang Guo,Roger Zimmermann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LightKV通过利用视觉token嵌入中的冗余性，采用文本提示引导的跨模态消息传递来压缩KV缓存，在仅保留55%原始视觉token的情况下，将视觉token的KV缓存减半、计算量减少40%，同时保持通用性能并显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代大型视觉-语言模型（LVLMs）在推理时直接采用KV缓存会导致大量GPU内存开销，因为预填充阶段需要处理大量视觉token。现有方法多采用纯视觉压缩策略，缺乏文本提示的引导。

Method: 提出LightKV，通过文本提示引导的跨模态消息传递，在预填充阶段逐步压缩视觉token，利用视觉token嵌入间的冗余性来减小KV缓存大小。

Result: 在8个开源LVLMs和8个公开基准数据集上评估，仅用55%的原始视觉token即可将视觉token的KV缓存减半，计算量减少40%，性能优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: LightKV是一种有效的视觉token KV缓存压缩方法，通过提示感知的跨模态压缩策略在内存效率和性能之间取得了良好平衡。

Abstract: Key-Value (KV) cache has become a de facto component of modern Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) for inference. While it enhances decoding efficiency in Large Language Models (LLMs), its direct adoption in LVLMs introduces substantial GPU memory overhead due to the large number of vision tokens processed during the prefill stage. To tackle this problem, we propose LightKV, a novel approach that reduces KV cache size by exploiting the redundancy among vision-token embeddings. Guided by text prompts, LightKV employs cross-modality message passing to aggregate informative messages across vision tokens and progressively compress them during prefill. This prompt-aware guidance distinguishes our method from prior vision-only compression strategies. We evaluate LightKV on eight open-source LVLMs across eight public benchmark datasets, e.g., MME and SeedBench. Experimental results demonstrate that with only 55% of the original vision tokens, LightKV (a) halves the vision-token KV cache size, (b) reduces computation by up to 40%, and (c) preserves general-purpose performance while significantly outperforming existing baselines.

</details>


### [47] [GMGaze: MoE-Based Context-Aware Gaze Estimation with CLIP and Multiscale Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00799)
*Xinyuan Zhao,Yihang Wu,Ahmad Chaddad,Sarah A. Alkhodair,Reem Kateb*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GMGaze，一种基于多尺度Transformer的注视估计方法。它利用语义原型调节CLIP全局嵌入生成两个上下文偏置全局token，并在第一层与CLIP patch和CNN token进行早期统一融合，同时引入稀疏专家混合模块实现条件计算容量。通过对抗域适应和特征分离损失促进全局token去相关。在四个基准数据集上取得SOTA结果，跨域评估也表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于CNN、Transformer和CLIP的注视估计方法存在三个主要挑战：图像特征晚期融合导致信息丢失、缺乏因子感知的条件调制、以及容量扩展不切实际。本文旨在解决这些问题，提出一种能早期融合多尺度特征、实现因子感知条件调制且容量扩展高效的注视估计方法。

Method: 提出GMGaze框架：1）语义原型调节（Semantic Prototype Conditioning）：利用四个原型库（光照、背景、头部姿态、外观）对CLIP全局图像嵌入进行调制，生成两个互补的上下文偏置全局token；2）早期统一融合：全局token与CLIP patch token和CNN token在第一层融合，避免晚期合并的信息损失；3）稀疏专家混合（MoE）模块：为每个token提供条件计算容量，无需均匀增加密集参数；4）跨域适应：采用对抗域适应技术，并引入特征分离损失鼓励两个全局token去相关。

Result: 在MPIIFaceGaze、EYEDIAP、Gaze360和ETH-XGaze四个基准上，平均角度误差分别达到2.49°、3.22°、10.16°和1.44°，在所有域内设置中优于先前基线。跨域评估中，在两个标准迁移路径上取得SOTA结果。

Conclusion: GMGaze通过早期统一融合、语义原型条件调制和稀疏MoE架构，有效解决了现有方法在特征融合、条件计算和容量扩展方面的三大挑战，在域内和跨域注视估计任务中均达到最优性能。

Abstract: Gaze estimation methods commonly use facial appearances to predict the direction of a person gaze. However, previous studies show three major challenges with convolutional neural network (CNN)-based, transformer-based, and contrastive language-image pre-training (CLIP)-based methods, including late fusion of image features, lack of factor-aware conditioning, and impractical capacity scaling. To address these challenges, we propose Globally-conditioned Multi-scale Gaze estimation (GMGaze), which leverages a multi-scale transformer architecture. Specifically, the model first introduces semantic prototype conditioning, which modulates the CLIP global image embedding using four learned prototype banks (i.e., illumination, background, head pose and appearance) to generate two complementary context-biased global tokens. These tokens, along with the CLIP patch and CNN tokens, are fused at the first layer. This early unified fusion prevents information loss common in late-stage merging. Finally, each token passes through sparse Mixture-of-Experts modules, providing conditional computational capacity without uniformly increasing dense parameters. For cross-domain adaptation, we incorporate an adversarial domain adaptation technique with a feature separation loss that encourages the two global tokens to remain de-correlated. Experiments using four public benchmarks (MPIIFaceGaze, EYEDIAP, Gaze360, and ETH-XGaze) show that GMGaze achieves mean angular errors of 2.49$^\circ$, 3.22$^\circ$, 10.16$^\circ$, and 1.44$^\circ$, respectively, outperforming previous baselines in all within-domain settings. In cross-domain evaluations, it provides state-of-the-art (SOTA) results on two standard transfer routes.

</details>


### [48] [Let ViT Speak: Generative Language-Image Pre-training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00809)
*Yan Fang,Mengcheng Lan,Zilong Huang,Weixian Lei,Yunqing Zhao,Yujie Zhong,Yingchen Yu,Qi She,Yao Zhao,Yunchao Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 GenLIP，一种用于多模态大语言模型的视觉编码器生成式预训练框架，通过让 ViT 直接预测语言 token 来对齐视觉与语言模态，无需对比学习或额外文本解码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉编码器与自回归 LLM 之间存在模态对齐不足的问题，需要更简单、可扩展且性能优越的预训练方法来桥接视觉与语言表征。

Method: 使用单一 Transformer 架构，以标准语言建模目标训练 ViT 直接从视觉 token 预测语言 token，无需对比批次构造或额外文本解码器；支持多分辨率、原生宽高比的继续预训练。

Result: 在 Recap-DataComp-1B 的 8B 样本上训练后，GenLIP 在多种多模态基准上匹配或超越强基线；继续预训练后，在 OCR 和图表理解等细节敏感任务上进一步提升。

Conclusion: GenLIP 是一种简单、可扩展且高效的视觉编码器预训练框架，适合作为 MLLM 的视觉基础编码器。

Abstract: In this paper, we present \textbf{Gen}erative \textbf{L}anguage-\textbf{I}mage \textbf{P}re-training (GenLIP), a minimalist generative pretraining framework for Vision Transformers (ViTs) designed for multimodal large language models (MLLMs). To better align vision encoders with the autoregressive nature of LLMs, GenLIP trains a ViT to predict language tokens directly from visual tokens using a standard language modeling objective, without contrastive batch construction or an additional text decoder. This design offers three key advantages: (1) \textbf{Simplicity}: a single transformer jointly models visual and textual tokens; (2) \textbf{Scalability}: it scales effectively with both data and model size; and (3) \textbf{Performance}: it achieves competitive or superior results across diverse multimodal benchmarks. Trained on 8B samples from Recap-DataComp-1B, GenLIP matches or surpasses strong baselines despite using substantially less pretraining data. After continued pretraining on multi-resolution images at native aspect ratios, GenLIP further improves on detail-sensitive tasks such as OCR and chart understanding, making it a strong foundation for vision encoders in MLLMs.

</details>


### [49] [Persistent Visual Memory: Sustaining Perception for Deep Generation in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00814)
*Siyuan Huang,Xiaoye Qu,Yafu Li,Tong Zhu,Zefeng He,Muxin Fu,Daizong Liu,Wei-Long Zheng,Yu Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对自回归LVLM中的"视觉信号稀释"问题（随生成长度增加，视觉注意力衰减），提出PVM（持久视觉记忆）模块，作为FFN的并行分支，建立不依赖距离的检索路径，直接提供视觉嵌入，缓解信号抑制。在Qwen3-VL上实验，以极小参数开销带来一致性能提升，尤其复杂推理任务，并能抵抗长度导致的信号衰减、加速内部预测收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归LVLM在长文本生成中，视觉注意力随序列长度增加而衰减，导致视觉信号被稀释，影响需要持续视觉感知的复杂推理任务。

Method: 提出PVM（Persistent Visual Memory）轻量可学习模块，作为FFN的并行分支集成到LVLM中，建立距离无关的检索路径，直接提供视觉嵌入，从结构上缓解深度生成中的信号抑制。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL的4B和8B规模上，PVM以极少的参数开销带来一致的准确率提升，尤其复杂推理任务；能抵抗长度引起的信号衰减并加速内部预测收敛。

Conclusion: PVM有效解决了视觉信号稀释问题，是一种轻量、高效的增强视觉持久感知的方案。

Abstract: While autoregressive Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) demonstrate remarkable proficiency in multimodal tasks, they face a "Visual Signal Dilution" phenomenon, where the accumulation of textual history expands the attention partition function, causing visual attention to decay inversely with generated sequence length. To counteract this, we propose Persistent Visual Memory (PVM), a lightweight learnable module designed to ensure sustained, on-demand visual perception. Integrated as a parallel branch alongside the Feed-Forward Network (FFN) in LVLMs, PVM establishes a distance-agnostic retrieval pathway that directly provides visual embeddings for precise visual perception, thereby structurally mitigating the signal suppression inherent to deep generation. Extensive experiments on Qwen3-VL models demonstrate that PVM brings notable improvements with negligible parameter overhead, delivering consistent average accuracy gains across both 4B and 8B scales, particularly in complex reasoning tasks that demand persistent visual perception. Furthermore, in-depth analysis reveals that PVM can resist length-induced signal decay and accelerate internal prediction convergence.

</details>


### [50] [Posterior Augmented Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00825)
*George Stoica,Sayak Paul,Matthew Wallingford,Vivek Ramanujan,Abhay Nori,Winson Han,Ali Farhadi,Ranjay Krishna,Judy Hoffman*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出后验增强流匹配（PAFM），通过用近似后验的期望替代单目标监督，解决了流匹配（FM）在高维图像生成中训练信号稀疏、方差大及流崩溃的问题。PAFM将难解后验分解为中间状态在假设终点下的似然与终点的条件先验之积，并通过重要性采样构建多候选目标混合。理论证明PAFM是FM目标的无偏估计且显著降低梯度方差，实验表明在SiT和MMDiT架构上、ImageNet和CC12M数据集上FID50K提升最高达3.4，计算开销几乎可忽略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准流匹配（FM）在高维图像生成中，每个训练样本仅监督单条轨迹和单个中间点，导致训练信号极度稀疏且方差大。这种欠约束的监督方式会引发流崩溃（flow collapse）——模型记忆特定源-目标配对，将多样输入映射到过于相似的输出，泛化能力差。

Method: PAFM提出用近似后验的期望替代单目标监督。具体而言，将难解的完整目标后验分解为：(i) 中间状态在假设终点下的似然，与 (ii) 该终点的条件先验之积；然后利用重要性采样构建多候选目标的混合分布，从而聚合每个中间状态对应的多条合理延续轨迹的信息。

Result: 在SiT-B/2和SiT-XL/2等多种模型规模、SiT和MMDiT两种架构、ImageNet（类别条件）和CC12M（文本条件）两个基准上，PAFM相比标准FM的FID50K提升最高达3.4，且计算开销增量可忽略不计。

Conclusion: PAFM是一种理论上完备的FM泛化方法，在不显著增加计算成本的前提下，有效缓解了高维图像生成中流匹配的稀疏监督和流崩溃问题，显著提升了生成质量。

Abstract: Flow matching (FM) trains a time-dependent vector field that transports samples from a simple prior to a complex data distribution. However, for high-dimensional images, each training sample supervises only a single trajectory and intermediate point, yielding an extremely sparse and high-variance training signal. This under-constrained supervision can cause flow collapse, where the learned dynamics memorize specific source-target pairings, mapping diverse inputs to overly similar outputs, failing to generalize. We introduce Posterior-Augmented Flow Matching (PAFM), a theoretically grounded generalization of FM that replaces single-target supervision with an expectation over an approximate posterior of valid target completions for a given intermediate state and condition. PAFM factorizes this intractable posterior into (i) the likelihood of the intermediate under a hypothesized endpoint and (ii) the prior probability of that endpoint under the condition, and uses an importance sampling scheme to construct a mixture over multiple candidate targets. We prove that PAFM yields an unbiased estimator of the original FM objective while substantially reducing gradient variance during training by aggregating information from many plausible continuation trajectories per intermediate. Finally, we show that PAFM improves over FM by up to 3.4 FID50K across different model scales (SiT-B/2 and SiT-XL/2), different architectures (SiT and MMDiT), and in both class and text conditioned benchmarks (ImageNet and CC12M), with a negligible increase in the compute overhead. Code: https://github.com/gstoica27/PAFM.git.

</details>

### [51] [Symbolic Execution Meets Multi-LLM Orchestration: Detecting Memory Vulnerabilities in Incomplete Rust CVE Snippets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00034)
*Zeyad Abdelrazek,Young Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合符号执行（KLEE）与四智能体多LLM架构的系统，用于检测Rust不安全代码中的内存漏洞。系统通过四个专门化智能体协作合成KLEE兼容的测试框架，解决了CVE不完整代码片段导致的编译失败问题。在31个真实Rust CVE上达到90.3%的包装编译成功率，检测出1,206个关键错误（83.9%检测率），显著优于现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式化验证工具在处理Rust不安全代码中的内存漏洞时面临挑战：CVE数据库条目仅提供不完整的代码片段（缺少结构体定义、导入和Cargo清单），导致所有现有形式化验证工具编译失败且零输出。需要一种能够从不完整代码片段中合成可执行测试框架的系统。

Method: 系统包含四个专门化智能体：Oracle/Validator（战略规划）、Safety Checker（漏洞分析）、Code Specialist（FFI包装生成器）和Fast Filter（执行优化），协同合成KLEE兼容的测试框架。KLEE输出由graph_klee.py处理，构建包含CVE文件、CWE类别、错误类型和符号执行路径的图数据库，支持结构化跨CVE漏洞查询。

Result: 在31个真实Rust CVE（涵盖11个CWE类别）上，包装编译成功率达90.3%（所有现有形式化验证工具为0%）。检测出1,206个关键错误（26个文件，83.9%检测率），而Clippy仅14个警告（11个文件，35.5%），Miri仅提供通用标签。四智能体架构将包装编译失败率从42%（单智能体基线）降至9.7%，检测错误数从487增至1,206。

Conclusion: 角色专门化和结构化上下文传递比单一通用模型产生可衡量的更好结果。四智能体多LLM架构有效解决了不完整代码问题，显著提升了Rust不安全代码的内存漏洞检测能力。复制包已在GitHub上公开。

Abstract: This paper presents a system combining symbolic execution (KLEE) with a 4-agent multi-LLM architecture for detecting memory vulnerabilities in Rust unsafe code. A central challenge we address is the incomplete-code problem: CVE database entries provide only isolated code snippets that lack struct definitions, imports, and Cargo manifests, causing all existing formal verification tools to fail at compilation with zero output. Our system resolves this through four specialized agents -- an Oracle/Validator for strategic planning, a Safety Checker for vulnerability analysis, a Code Specialist for FFI wrapper generation, and a Fast Filter for execution optimization -- that collaboratively synthesize KLEE-compatible harnesses from otherwise uncompilable fragments. KLEE's output is then ingested by graph_klee.py, which constructs a Graph Database linking CVE files, CWE categories, error types, and symbolic execution paths as typed nodes and labelled edges, enabling structured cross-CVE vulnerability queries. We evaluated our system on 31 real-world Rust CVEs spanning 11 CWE categories, achieving 90.3% wrapper compilation success where all state-of-the-art formal verification tools achieve 0%. Our system detected 1,206 critical errors across 26 files (83.9% detection rate), compared to 14 warnings across 11 files for Clippy (35.5%) and generic labels for Miri. The 4-agent architecture reduced wrapper compilation failures from 42% (single-agent baseline) to 9.7% and increased detected errors from 487 to 1,206, confirming that role specialization and structured context passing produce measurably better results than a single general-purpose model. Our replication package is publicly available at https://github.com/Zeyad-Ab/Symbolic-Execution-with-Multi-LLM-Architecture-for-Rust-Security

</details>


### [52] [zkSBOM: Privacy-Preserving SBOM Sharing with Zero-Knowledge Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00076)
*Tom Sorger,Eric Cornelissen,Aman Sharma,Javier Ron,Musard Balliu,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出zkSBOM，一种基于零知识集合的隐私保护型软件物料清单（SBOM）共享机制，允许消费者在不泄露额外SBOM内容的情况下验证漏洞存在性，并同时保护供应商和消费者免受对方攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SBOM共享机制面临二元选择：要么完全公开（使供应商面临从SBOM推断出的攻击风险，如暴露存在漏洞的依赖项），要么完全不公开（使消费者无法验证SBOM真实性，可能被篡改或虚假SBOM欺骗）。需要一种既能保护供应商隐私又能保障消费者验证能力的机制。

Method: zkSBOM利用零 knowledge 集合（zero-knowledge sets）对SBOM中的组件进行密码学承诺。消费者针对已知漏洞发起查询，获得一个密码学证明，该证明仅确认SBOM描述的工件是否受该漏洞影响，而不透露任何额外的SBOM内容。

Result: 通过量化包含证明和排除证明的预期信息泄漏进行了安全性分析，并在真实场景中验证了可行性，评估了运行需求。实验表明zkSBOM是一种强健、安全且保护隐私的SBOM共享机制。

Conclusion: zkSBOM有效解决了SBOM共享中隐私与透明度之间的冲突，为软件供应链安全提供了一种平衡供应商隐私保护和消费者验证需求的实用方案。

Abstract: Software Bills of Materials (SBOMs) are increasingly mandated by regulators, yet existing sharing mechanisms impose a binary choice between full disclosure and full opacity. This exposes software suppliers to attacks that can be deduced from the SBOM only, such as the presence of a vulnerable dependency. Conversely, software consumers can be fooled by software suppliers who modify or misrepresent published SBOMs. We present zkSBOM, a privacy-preserving SBOM sharing mechanism designed to address these threats. zkSBOM uses zero-knowledge sets to cryptographically commit to the components within an SBOM. Software consumers can query for known vulnerabilities and receive a cryptographic proof confirming whether the artifact described by the SBOM is affected, without revealing any additional SBOM content. We conduct a security analysis of zkSBOM by quantifying expected leakage from inclusion and exclusion proofs. We demonstrate real-world feasibility by applying it to realistic scenarios and evaluating its operation requirements. Our evaluation demonstrates that zkSBOM is a strong, secure, and privacy-preserving mechanism for SBOM sharing, protecting software suppliers and software consumers from one another.

</details>


### [53] [I can't recognize (yet): Delayed Rendering to Defeat Visual Phishing Detectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00183)
*Ying Yuan,Cristiano Alex Rado,Giovanni Apruzzese,Mauro Conti,Luigi Vincenzo Mancini*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次揭示基于视觉相似性的钓鱼网页检测系统存在一种时序攻击漏洞：攻击者通过延迟渲染关键元素（如Logo），使检测器在截图时获取不完整的页面视觉信息，从而绕过检测。实验表明，该方法可使SOTA检测器的检测率从100%降至0%，且用户难以察觉。论文还提出了一种无需远程调用的浏览器扩展作为缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管视觉机器学习方法在钓鱼网页检测中表现出色，但这类防御系统固有的时序攻击脆弱性从未被系统研究过，这种疏忽威胁到其在实际部署中的可靠性。

Method: 1) 揭示时序规避攻击的存在及原理：延迟渲染网页关键元素（如Logo）使其在截图后才完整显示；2) 通过问题空间技术（如帘幕效果）对SOTA检测器进行实证攻击；3) 开展用户研究评估攻击对人类用户的隐蔽性；4) 提出基于浏览器扩展的缓解方案。

Result: 时序攻击可使SOTA视觉钓鱼检测器的检测率从100%降至0%；用户研究显示真实用户无法可靠识别这些扰动（p<.05）。

Conclusion: 基于视觉相似性的钓鱼检测系统本质上易受时序规避攻击，亟需新的防御机制，论文提出的无远程调用的浏览器扩展是一种可行的缓解方向。

Abstract: Phishing webpages are continuously polluting the Web. Plenty of countermeasures have been proposed and the most advanced techniques leverage machine-learning methods that infer whether a webpage is benign or not by inspecting its visual representation. Yet, despite the demonstrated effectiveness of such detection methods, this class of defenses is, by design, susceptible to a kind of subtle-but-cheap timing-based attacks which -- worryingly, and perhaps surprisingly -- have never been investigated so far. Such an oversight questions the overall reliability of these defenses in the wild.
  First, we show that timing-based evasion attacks have not been accounted for by prior work on visual phishing websites detectors. Then, we elucidate the intrinsic vulnerability of these detectors: they can be bypassed by delaying the rendering of webpage elements. Practically, these detectors must compute the visual similarity between a target webpage and a known legitimate one. This requires taking a "snapshot" of the target webpage before the similarity computation. Attackers can deliberately delay the rendering of key elements, such as the logo, so that these elements appear fully only after the snapshot has been taken. This simple tactic misleads the visual-similarity module, leading the system to incorrectly classify the phishing page as benign. We empirically show that state-of-the-art detectors can be completely defeated (detection rate dropping from 100% to 0%) by employing easy-to-apply problem-space techniques such as curtain effects. We also carry out a user study, evaluating the effectiveness of these attacks against real humans, and find that end users are unable to reliably identify our "perturbations" (p<.05). Finally, we propose mitigations, including a browser-extension that, without making any call to remote services, warns users that they may have landed on a phishing webpage.

</details>


### [54] [Attention Is Where You Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00236)
*Aviral Srivastava,Sourav Panda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出注意力重分布攻击（ARA），通过识别安全关键的注意力头并利用Gumbel-softmax优化重定向其注意力分布，以极少的对抗标记（5个）和优化步数（500步）绕过LLM的安全对齐。实验表明，安全行为并非局部化于特定注意力头作为可移除组件，而是源于这些头所执行的注意力路由机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全对齐的大型语言模型依赖RLHF和指令微调来拒绝有害请求，但实现安全行为的内部机制尚不清晰。现有越狱方法多在语义或输出logit层面操作，缺乏对注意力机制几何结构的理解与利用。

Method: 提出注意力重分布攻击（ARA），分为两步：(1) 识别安全关键的注意力头；(2) 通过Gumbel-softmax优化，在概率单纯形上重定向这些头的注意力分布，生成非语义对抗标记。

Result: 在LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct、Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.1和Gemma-2-9B-it上，ARA分别达到30%、36%和1%的攻击成功率。关键发现：消融安全头几乎不影响拒绝行为（最多1次翻转），而重定向其注意力则显著破坏安全（Mistral上72/200、LLaMA上60/200翻转）。

Conclusion: 安全行为并非局部化于特定注意力头作为可移除组件，而是源于注意力路由机制。移除头部可通过残差流补偿，而重定向其注意力会向下游传播损坏信号。这一发现揭示了安全对齐的新脆弱性维度。

Abstract: Safety-aligned large language models rely on RLHF and instruction tuning to refuse harmful requests, yet the internal mechanisms implementing safety behavior remain poorly understood. We introduce the Attention Redistribution Attack (ARA), a white-box adversarial attack that identifies safety-critical attention heads and crafts nonsemantic adversarial tokens that redirect attention away from safety-relevant positions. Unlike prior jailbreak methods operating at the semantic or output-logit level, ARA targets the geometry of softmax attention on the probability simplex using Gumbel-softmax optimization over targeted heads. Across LLaMA-3-8B-Instruct, Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.1, and Gemma-2-9B-it, ARA bypasses safety alignment with as few as 5 tokens and 500 optimization steps, achieving 36% ASR on Mistral-7B and 30% on LLaMA-3 against 200 HarmBench prompts, while Gemma-2 remains at 1%. Our principal mechanistic finding is a dissociation between ablation and redistribution: zeroing out the top-ranked safety heads produces at most 1 flip among 39 to 50 baseline refusals, while ARA targeting the corresponding safety-heavy layers flips 72/200 prompts on Mistral-7B and 60/200 on LLaMA-3. This suggests that safety is not localized in these heads as removable components, but emerges from the attention routing they perform. Removing a head allows compensation through the residual stream, while redirecting its attention propagates a corrupted signal downstream.

</details>


### [55] [A Privacy-Preserving Approach to Conformance Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00283)
*Luis Rodríguez-Flores,Luciano García-Bañuelos,Abel Armas-Cervantes,Astrid Rivera-Partida*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于同态加密和字符串处理算法的安全一致性检查方法，允许在模型和日志均不公开的情况下计算对齐，但代价是高昂的内存和处理开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统一致性检查假设过程模型和事件日志对分析师均可见，但在某些场景下模型或日志包含敏感信息需要保密，因此需要一种能够在不暴露双方数据的前提下进行一致性检查的隐私保护方法。

Method: 基于对齐（alignments）这一一致性检查的经典形式化方法，结合字符串处理算法与同态加密技术，使得模型所有者和日志所有者各自加密自己的数据，在密文上进行对齐计算，双方均无法看到对方的原始数据。

Result: 在合成日志和真实世界事件日志上的实验表明，该方法能够安全地完成一致性检查，但需要高昂的内存和计算资源。

Conclusion: 安全一致性检查在理论上是可行的，但当前方案的性能开销较大，未来需要优化以提升实用性。

Abstract: Conformance checking, one of the main process mining operations, aims to identify discrepancies between a process model and an event log. The model represents the expected behaviour, whereas the event log represents the actual process behaviour as captured in information systems records. Traditionally, the process model and the event log are both accessible to the business analyst performing the conformance checking. However, in some contexts, it is necessary to keep either the model or the log private to protect critical or sensitive information. In this paper, we propose a secure approach to conformance checking based on string processing algorithms and homomorphic encryption, where the process model and event log ar not visible to either the model's or event log's owner. The proposed technique is based on alignments, a well-known formalism used for conformance checking. An evaluation is performed using a synthetic and a real-world event log, showing that conformance checking can be securely computed at the expense of high memory and processing requirements.

</details>


### [56] [Trident: Improving Malware Detection with LLMs and Behavioral Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00297)
*Rebecca Saul,Jingzhi Jiang,Elliott Chia,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出利用大语言模型(LLM)处理沙箱行为报告生成行为检测规则，并与静态特征决策树模型结合构建Trident系统，相比传统静态特征方法对概念漂移具有更强鲁棒性，且无需重训练即可达到主动学习方法的抗漂移水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PE恶意软件检测依赖字节直方图、字符串信息等静态特征，而动态分析特征因沙箱行为报告的半结构化特性难以整合。作者希望利用LLM突破这一障碍，提升检测对概念漂移的鲁棒性。

Method: 1) 利用具备推理能力的大语言模型，基于少量标注恶意样本生成行为检测规则；2) 构建Trident系统，融合三个组件：基于静态特征的经典决策树模型、LLM生成的行为检测规则、以及通过多数投票对沙箱报告进行直接LLM分析。

Result: 行为检测规则相比静态特征方法对概念漂移的鲁棒性更强，同时保持实用的假阳性率；Trident系统优于纯静态特征方法，优于单独的行为规则，且在无需重训练的情况下达到与主动学习方法相当的概念漂移抗性。

Conclusion: LLM能够有效处理半结构化的沙箱行为报告并生成鲁棒的检测规则；将静态与动态分析结合的混合系统（Trident）在准确性和抗概念漂移能力上均优于传统方法。

Abstract: Traditionally, machine learning methods for PE malware detection have relied on static features like byte histograms, string information, and PE header contents. One barrier to incorporating dynamic analysis features has been the semi-structured nature of sandbox behavior reports. We show that, using the latest generation of large language models with reasoning, it is possible to efficiently process these behavior reports and utilize them as part of a malware detection pipeline. Specifically, we leverage LLMs to generate behavior-based malware detection rules based on a small training set of labeled malware. We find that these detection rules, derived from behavioral features, are much more robust to concept drift than standard static-feature methods, while maintaining practical false positive rates. Finally, we introduce Trident, a system which combines a classic decision tree model over static features, our behavior-based detection rules, and direct LLM analysis of sandbox reports through majority voting. Trident outperforms standard methods using static features, outperforms behavior-based rules alone, and is as resilient to concept drift as active learning methods without requiring retraining.

</details>


### [57] [Block-wise Codeword Embedding for Reliable Multi-bit Text Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00348)
*Joeun Kim,HoEun Kim,Dongsup Jin,Young-Sik Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: BREW提出了一种基于分块可靠嵌入的水印框架，通过盲消息估计和窗口移位验证解决多比特水印中的高假阳性问题，在10%同义词替换下实现了0.965的TPR和0.02的FPR。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于ECC的多比特水印提取器存在灾难性的假阳性率（FPR），且施加拒绝阈值会导致检测灵敏度（TPR）退化为随机猜测。作者旨在证明高FPR并非多比特水印的固有权衡，而是可解决的结构性缺陷。

Method: 提出BREW框架，包含两阶段机制：(i) 通过独立块投票实现盲消息估计；(ii) 通过窗口移位验证严格校验载荷对局部编辑的鲁棒性。

Result: 在10%同义词替换攻击下，BREW达到0.965的真阳性率（TPR）和0.02的假阳性率（FPR）。

Conclusion: 高FPR问题不是多比特水印的固有折衷，而是先前以解码为中心的设计中的结构性缺陷。BREW是模型无关且理论完备的，为可靠的取证部署提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent multi-bit watermarking methods for large language models (LLMs) prioritize capacity over reliability, often conflating decoding with detection. Our analysis reveals that existing ECC-based extractors suffer from catastrophic false positive rates (FPR), and applying rejection thresholds merely collapses detection sensitivity (TPR) to random guessing. To resolve this structural limitation, we propose \textbf{BREW} (Block-wise Reliable Embedding for Watermarking), a framework shifting the paradigm to \emph{designated verification}. BREW employs a two-stage mechanism: (i) \textbf{blind message estimation} via independent block voting, followed by (ii) \textbf{window-shifting verification} that rigorously validates the payload against local edits. Experiments demonstrate that BREW achieves a TPR of 0.965 with an FPR of 0.02 under 10\% synonym substitution, demonstrating that the high-FPR issue is not an inherent trade-off of multi-bit watermarking, but a solvable structural flaw of prior decoding-centric designs. Our framework is model-agnostic and theoretically grounded, providing a scalable solution for reliable forensic deployment.

</details>


### [58] [Skills as Verifiable Artifacts: A Trust Schema and a Biconditional Correctness Criterion for Human-in-the-Loop Agent Runtimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00424)
*Alfredo Metere*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种技能验证框架，将AI Agent技能视为非可信代码，通过显式的验证级别、能力门控和双条件正确性标准，实现可持续的人机协同安全执行，无需重新训练模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI Agent技能（结构化指令包）已从便利性工具演变为一级部署制品，但运行时加载技能时面临类似操作系统包管理器的信任问题：技能声称某种行为，运行时必须判断是否可信。传统基于签名、权限或注册表的隐式信任机制不足以应对这一挑战。

Method: 本文提出：(1) 信任模式（§\ref{sec:schema}），在技能清单中显式包含验证级别；(2) 能力门控（§\ref{sec:gate}），人机协同策略是验证级别的函数；(3) 双条件正确性标准（§\ref{sec:biconditional}），验证过程需在对抗性集成测试中满足；(4) 可移植运行时配置文件（§\ref{sec:guidelines}），包含十条规范性指南，来自开源参考实现。

Result: 提出了一个与模型无关、与框架无关的验证框架。在技能验证作为独立门控流程的情况下，人机协同仅在未验证的操作上触发，使系统变得可持续。验证过程需通过对抗性集成测试来满足双条件正确性标准。

Conclusion: 技能应被视为非可信代码直至被验证，运行时必须强制执行此默认规则而非依赖签名、权限或注册表的隐式信任。通过将验证作为独立门控流程，系统可在规模化部署中保持可持续，无需重新训练、微调或专有基础设施。

Abstract: Agent skills -- structured packages of instructions, scripts, and references that augment a large language model (LLM) without modifying the model itself -- have moved from convenience to first-class deployment artifact. The runtime that loads them inherits the same problem package managers and operating systems have always faced: a piece of content claims a behavior; the runtime must decide whether to believe it. We argue this paper's central thesis up front: a skill is \emph{untrusted code} until it is verified, and the runtime that loads it must enforce that default rather than infer trust from a signature, a clearance, or a registry of origin. Without skill verification, a human-in-the-loop (HITL) gate must fire on every irreversible call -- which is operationally untenable and degrades into rubber-stamping at any non-trivial scale. With skill verification treated as a separate, gated process, HITL fires only for what is unverified, and the system becomes sustainable. We give a trust schema (§\ref{sec:schema}) that includes an explicit verification level on every skill manifest; a capability gate (§\ref{sec:gate}) whose HITL policy is a function of that verification level; a \emph{biconditional} correctness criterion (§\ref{sec:biconditional}) that any candidate verification procedure must satisfy on an adversarial-ensemble exercise (§\ref{sec:eval}); and a portable runtime profile (§\ref{sec:guidelines}) with ten normative guidelines abstracted from a working open-source reference implementation \cite{metere2026enclawed}. The contribution is harness- and model-agnostic; nothing here requires retraining, fine-tuning, or proprietary infrastructure.

</details>


### [59] [CleanBase: Detecting Malicious Documents in RAG Knowledge Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00460)
*Weifei Jin,Xilong Wang,Wei Zou,Jinyuan Jia,Neil Gong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CleanBase是一种通过构建文档相似度图并检测恶意文档形成的团簇来防御RAG系统提示注入攻击的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检索增强生成（RAG）系统易受提示注入攻击，攻击者将精心构造的恶意文档注入知识库，当用户提出被攻击的目标问题时，RAG系统可能检索到这些恶意文档，其注入的提示误导系统生成攻击者指定的回答，破坏了RAG系统的完整性。

Method: CleanBase的关键洞察是：针对同一攻击目标问题构造的恶意文档具有高度语义相似性。基于此，CleanBase在知识库上构建相似度图，每个节点代表一个文档，若两个文档的语义相似度（使用嵌入模型计算）超过统计确定的阈值，则连接一条边。由于内在相似性，恶意文档倾向于在图中形成团簇。CleanBase检测这些团簇并将对应文档标记为恶意。作者理论上推导了CleanBase假阳性和假阴性率的上界。

Result: 在多个数据集和多种提示注入攻击下的实验结果表明，CleanBase能够准确检测恶意文档，有效保护RAG系统。

Conclusion: CleanBase通过利用恶意文档针对同一攻击目标问题时的语义相似性，构建相似度图并检测团簇，有效防御了RAG系统的提示注入攻击，并从理论和实验两方面验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is vulnerable to prompt injection attacks, in which an adversary inserts malicious documents containing carefully crafted injected prompts into the knowledge database. When a user issues a question targeted by the attack, the RAG system may retrieve these malicious documents, whose injected prompts mislead it into generating attacker-specified answers, thereby compromising the integrity of the RAG system. In this work, we propose CleanBase, a method to detect malicious documents within a knowledge database. Our key insight is that malicious documents crafted for the same attack-targeted questions often exhibit high semantic similarity, as attackers deliberately make them consistent to improve attack success rates. Accordingly, CleanBase constructs a similarity graph over the knowledge database, where each node represents a document and an edge connects two nodes if their semantic similarity--computed using an embedding model--exceeds a statistically determined threshold. Due to their inherent similarity, malicious documents tend to form cliques within this graph. CleanBase detects such cliques and flags the corresponding documents as malicious. We theoretically derive upper bounds on CleanBase's false positive and false negative rates and empirically validate its effectiveness. Experimental results across multiple datasets and prompt injection attacks demonstrate that CleanBase accurately detects malicious documents and effectively safeguards RAG systems. Our source code is available at https://github.com/WeifeiJin/CleanBase.

</details>


### [60] [Zero-Knowledge Model Checking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00487)
*Pascal Berrang,Mirco Giacobbe,Jacob Swales,Xiao Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种零知识模型检验方案（显式状态和符号化），通过将演绎模型检验与零知识证明结合，使软件系统能够在不泄露自身代码的前提下，向验证者证明其满足公开的线性时序逻辑规范。原型实验验证了方案的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全攸关领域中，软件系统既需要满足功能正确性规范，又需要保护系统本身的机密性（如商业机密或军事代码）。现有形式化验证技术通常需要完全公开系统代码，无法同时满足安全性与保密性需求。

Method: 1) 将演绎模型检验的证明证书（排名函数）与零知识证明结合；2) 显式状态方案：系统表示为转移图，使用多项式承诺证明公开的证明证书对应于秘密的转移关系；3) 符号化方案：系统表示为线性守卫命令，使用分段线性排名函数，通过Farkas引理获得含公开和秘密组件的排名函数有效性的见证，并采用sigma协议（矩阵乘法与范围证明）证明见证的存在性。

Result: 在原型系统上对线性时序逻辑验证示例进行了实验，证明了两种方案的实际有效性。

Conclusion: 该技术能够在安全性和保密性都至关重要的领域中实现形式化验证，为"不透明的正确性证明"提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: We introduce a technology to formally verify that a software system satisfies a temporal specification of functional correctness, without revealing the system itself. Our method combines a deductive approach to model checking to obtain a formal certificate of correctness for the system, with zero-knowledge proofs to convince an external verifier that the system -- kept secret -- complies with its specification of correctness -- made public. We consider proof certificates represented as ranking functions, and introduce both an explicit-state and a symbolic scheme for model checking in zero knowledge. Our explicit-state scheme assumes systems represented as transition graphs. We use polynomial commitments to convince the verifier that the public proof certificates correspond to the secret transition relation. Our symbolic scheme assumes systems specified as linear guarded commands and uses piecewise-linear ranking functions. We apply Farkas' lemma to obtain a witness for the validity of the ranking function with public and secret components, and employ sigma protocols for matrix multiplication and range proofs to convince the verifier of the witness's existence. We built a prototype to demonstrate the practical efficacy of our two schemes on linear temporal logic verification examples. Our technology enables formal verification in domains where both the safety and the confidentiality of the system under analysis are critical.

</details>


### [61] [Pick and Sort for Graphical Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00558)
*Argianto Rahartomo,AmirHossein Jamshidipoor,Mohammad Ghafari*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种“挑选并排序”（Pick and Sort）的图形化身份认证方案，用户通过选择视觉元素并在网格中排列来完成认证。该方案支持配置元素数量和网格大小，并可为儿童等特定用户群体定制视觉元素。初步研究表明该方案易于学习且部署灵活，虽然登录时间比传统方法长，但在非时间敏感场景（如低频访问或二次认证）中可接受。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统文本密码存在记忆负担和安全问题，而现有图形密码方案往往缺乏灵活性和用户定制能力。作者希望设计一种既安全又易用、且能适应不同用户群体（尤其是儿童）的图形化认证方案。

Method: 采用“挑选并排序”设计：用户从一组视觉元素中选择特定数量的元素，然后按照自定义顺序将其排列到网格中。方案允许配置所选元素数量和网格大小，并支持针对特定用户群体（如儿童）定制视觉元素。通过原型实现进行了初步的用户研究。

Result: 初步研究表明该方案易于学习和部署，但登录时间比传统认证方法更长。作者认为在非时间关键场景（如低频访问或作为二次认证机制）中，额外的交互时间是可以接受的。

Conclusion: 该图形化认证方案在易用性和灵活性方面具有优势，特别适合儿童等特殊用户群体以及在时间不敏感的场景中作为替代或补充认证方式。

Abstract: We propose a graphical authentication scheme that follows a simple ``Pick and Sort'' design in which users choose visual elements and arrange them within a grid. Both the number of selected elements and the grid size are configurable, and the visual elements can be customized for specific user groups, such as children. A preliminary study with a prototype implementation indicated that the scheme is easy to learn and flexible to deploy. Although login times are longer than those of conventional authentication methods, the additional interaction may be acceptable in scenarios that are not time-critical, such as infrequent-access use cases or as a secondary authentication mechanism.

</details>


### [62] [KingsGuard: Enclave Data Protection Under Real-World TEE Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00613)
*Saltanat Firdous Allaqband,Deepanjali S,Rohit Srinivas R G,Devashish Gosain,Chester Rebeiro*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: KINGSGUARD 是一种新型 TEE 设计，通过在硬件层面实施细粒度数据流追踪和检查，确保敏感数据不会离开飞地边界，并在飞地边界引入受控降级机制以平衡安全性与功能性。在 RISC-V 处理器上的 FPGA 综合实现显示硬件面积开销为 10.8%，性能开销为 5.69%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 TEE 的飞地代码和底层硬件设计存在漏洞，导致强隔离保证下敏感数据仍可能泄露，即理想威胁模型与实际实现之间存在差距。

Method: 提出 KINGSGUARD 设计，在硬件层面实施细粒度的数据流追踪和检查，强制敏感数据不离开飞地边界；同时引入受控降级机制（controlled declassification），允许在飞地边界有意释放数据。

Result: 在 RISC-V 处理器上 FPGA 综合实现，硬件面积开销 10.8%，性能开销 5.69%。

Conclusion: KINGSGUARD 通过硬件数据流追踪有效弥合了 TEE 理想威胁模型与实际实现之间的差距，以可接受的开销提供了更强的安全保障。

Abstract: Trusted Execution Environments (TEEs) have emerged as a cornerstone for securing sensitive computations by providing isolated enclaves protected from untrusted software. However, their security guarantees are undermined by vulnerabilities in both the enclave code and the underlying hardware design, which can allow sensitive data to leak despite strong isolation guarantees. This paper presents KINGSGUARD, a novel TEE design that systematically monitors and controls the propagation of sensitive data within an enclave. By enforcing fine-grained data flow tracking and checks in hardware, our approach ensures that sensitive data does not leave the enclave boundary, thus bridging the gap between the idealized threat models of TEEs and their practical realizations. Additionally, to balance security with practical functionality, we introduce controlled declassification at enclave boundaries, allowing intentional release of data to the outside world. Our implementation of KINGSGUARD on a RISC-V processor has a 10.8% hardware area overhead when synthesized on FPGA and a 5.69% performance overhead.

</details>


### [63] [Defense against Poisoning Attacks under Shuffle-DP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00625)
*Siyi Wang,Qiyao Luo,Yihua Hu,Lixu Wang,Quanqing Xu,Chuanhui Yang,Zhan Qin,Kui Ren,Wei Dong*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个针对所有并保留查询（union-preserving queries）的通用防御框架，可将任意洗牌差分隐私协议转换为抗投毒攻击的版本，在保证隐私的同时实现高效用，且误差与原始协议渐近等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有洗牌差分隐私协议默认所有用户诚实，无法抵御恶意用户的投毒攻击；已有的防御方案仅适用于频率估计任务，缺乏通用性。

Method: 提出通用防御框架，适用于所有并保留查询，可将任意洗牌DP协议转换为抗投毒攻击的版本；在无攻击时保持渐近等价误差，在常数个攻击者时仅增加多对数级误差。

Result: 在求和、频率估计、范围计数等多种常见查询上验证了框架的通用性；实验表明该方法能有效防御投毒攻击，同时保持强效用和通信效率。

Conclusion: 该工作是首个针对洗牌DP模型下投毒攻击的通用防御方案，填补了现有方法仅适用于频率估计的空白，为实际部署提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Differential Privacy (DP) has become the gold standard for protecting individual privacy in data analytics, and the shuffle-DP model has attracted significant attention from both academia and industry due to its favorable balance between privacy and utility. However, existing shuffle-DP protocols rely on a strong assumption: all users behave honestly. In real-world scenarios, adversarial users can exploit this vulnerability through poisoning attacks, compromising both privacy guarantees and the utility of analytical results. While defending against poisoning attacks in the shuffle-DP model has recently gained interest, existing solutions are limited to frequency estimation tasks. To address this issue, we propose the first general defense framework for all union-preserving queries, capable of transforming any shuffle-DP protocol into a version resilient to poisoning attacks. Beyond robust defense against poisoning attacks, our framework achieves high utility of analytical results. Compared to the original shuffle-DP protocol, it retains asymptotically equivalent error in attack-free settings and incurs only a polylogarithmic increase in error when a constant number of attackers are present. We demonstrate the generality of our framework on several common queries, including summation, frequency estimation, and range counting. Experimental results confirm that our approach effectively defends against poisoning attacks while maintaining strong utility and communication efficiency.

</details>


### [64] [STARE: Step-wise Temporal Alignment and Red-teaming Engine for Multi-modal Toxicity Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00699)
*Xutao Mao,Liangjie Zhao,Tao Liu,Xiang Zheng,Hongying Zan,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: STARE是一个层次强化学习框架，通过将去噪轨迹作为攻击面，在白盒T2I和黑盒VLM设置下实现了68%的攻击成功率提升，并发现了**优化诱导的相位对齐**现象——毒性生成在去噪过程的早期语义阶段和后期细化阶段呈现规律性分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有红队方法将图像生成视为黑盒，仅返回终端毒性分数，无法回答多步合成过程中毒性语义何时以及如何出现。作者希望打开去噪轨迹这一"黑箱"，理解毒性在扩散模型生成过程中的时间结构。

Method: 提出STARE框架，采用层次强化学习：(1)高层提示编辑器进行文本扰动；(2)低层使用GRPO对T2I模型进行细粒度微调，直接操纵去噪轨迹。在白盒T2I和查询仅黑盒VLM设置下工作。

Result: STARE相比最先进的基線实现了68%的攻击成功率提升。更重要的是发现了优化诱导的相位对齐现象：原始模型毒性弥散分布，对抗优化将概念性伤害集中在早期语义阶段，细节性伤害集中在后期细化阶段。

Conclusion: 毒性形成从混沌过程转变为可预测的脆弱性窗口，这一时间结构是真正的因果手柄而非层次设计的副作用。为相位感知的安全机制提供了基础。

Abstract: Red-teaming Vision-Language Models is essential for identifying vulnerabilities where adversarial image-text inputs trigger toxic outputs. Existing approaches treat image generation as a black box, returning only terminal toxicity scores and leaving open the question of when and how toxic semantics emerge during multi-step synthesis. We introduce STARE, a hierarchical reinforcement learning framework that treats the denoising trajectory itself as the attack surface, under a direct white-box T2I and query-only black-box VLM setting. By coupling a high-level prompt editor with low-level T2I fine-tuning via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), STARE attains a 68\% improvement in Attack Success Rate over state-of-the-art black-box and white-box baselines. More importantly, this trajectory-level view surfaces the Optimization-Induced Phase Alignment phenomenon: vanilla models exhibit diffuse toxicity, whereas adversarial optimization concentrates conceptual harms into early semantic phases and detail-oriented harms into late refinement. Targeted perturbations of either window selectively suppress different toxicity categories, indicating that this temporal structure is a genuine causal handle rather than a side effect of the hierarchical design. The phenomenon turns toxicity formation from a chaotic process into a small set of predictable vulnerability windows, providing both a potent attack engine and a basis for phase-aware safety mechanisms. Content warning: This paper contains examples of toxic content that may be offensive or disturbing.

</details>


### [65] [Self-Adaptive Multi-Agent LLM-Based Security Pattern Selection for IoT Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00741)
*Saeid Jamshidi,Foutse Khomh,Carol Fung,Kawser Wazed Nafi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ASPO是一种将LLM推理与确定性执行相结合的自适应多智能体安全模式选择框架，在资源受限的边缘IoT环境中实现了100%无冲突激活、资源可行性保障，并将尾部延迟和能耗开销分别降低21.9%和23.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘IoT系统中运行时防御机制从单纯检测转向资源约束下的缓解动作选择，现有基于静态规则和策略的方法在可行性、冲突安全和执行正确性方面缺乏保障。

Method: 提出ASPO框架，基于MAPE-K控制环，将LLM智能体用于随机决策生成（提出候选缓解组合），确定性优化核心用于执行（确保动作完整性、无冲突组合和资源可行性）。

Result: 在分布式边缘网关测试台上，基于1000次运行时安全决策评估，实现了100%无冲突激活、资源可行性一致保障，尾部延迟降低21.9%，能耗开销降低23.1%，且平均能耗未增加。

Conclusion: 分离随机决策生成与确定性执行的方法能有效保障边缘IoT场景下安全控制的可行性、安全性和正确性，同时降低延迟和能耗开销。

Abstract: The adoption of Internet of Things (IoT) systems at the network edge of smart architectures is increasing rapidly, intensifying the need for security mechanisms that are both adaptive and resource-efficient. In such environments, runtime defence mechanisms are no longer limited to detection alone but become a resource-constrained task of selecting mitigation actions. Security controls must be carefully selected, combined, and executed under latency, energy, and computational constraints, while preventing unsafe interactions between controls. Existing approaches predominantly rely on static rule sets and learned policies, which provide limited guarantees of feasibility, conflict safety, and execution correctness in resource-constrained edge settings. To address this limitation, we introduce ASPO, a self-adaptive multi-agent security pattern selection that integrates Large Language Model (LLM)-based reasoning with deterministic enforcement within a MAPE-K control loop. ASPO explicitly separates stochastic decision generation from execution: LLM agents propose candidate mitigation portfolios, while a deterministic optimisation core enforces closed-world action integrity, conflict-free composition, and resource feasibility at every decision epoch. We deploy ASPO on a distributed edge-gateway testbed and evaluate it across two workloads, each comprising 500 and 1000 runtime security decisions, using replayed IoT attack traffic. In addition, the results demonstrate invariant safety properties, including 100% conflict-free activation, consistent resource feasibility across workloads, and stable pattern dominance with perfect rank preservation. Importantly, deeper decision exploration reduces extreme-case execution costs, compressing tail latency and energy overheads by 21.9% and 23.1%, respectively, without increasing mean energy consumption.

</details>


### [66] [Repurposing Image Diffusion Models for Adversarial Synthetic Structured Data: A Case Study of Ground Truth Drift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00788)
*Adam Arthur,Christopher Schwartz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文测试了未经修改的Stable Diffusion U-Net在表格数据集（UCI Adult Income）上的应用，通过将每行数据重塑为伪图像来生成合成数据。研究发现模型的空间归纳偏置使得特征排布成为设计变量。论文还提出了两个哲学区分：统计真实性与感知真实性，以及合成证据与合成媒体的区别。攻击者可通过"站在接收方机器角度思考"成功制作合成证据，并可能引发"真实漂移"——AI生成内容在未审查来源的流水线中被重新分类为真实数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证无需训练专用表格数据生成器的攻击者是否可以直接利用现成的公开图像扩散模型（Stable Diffusion）来生成表格型合成数据，并探讨由此引发的认识论和安全性问题。

Method: 将UCI Adult Income数据集的每行记录重塑为小尺寸单通道伪图像，使用未经修改的Stable Diffusion U-Net进行处理，测试多种特征布局方式以应对模型对空间局部性的归纳偏置。

Result: 模型的空间归纳偏置使得特征排布成为影响合成质量的关键设计变量；攻击者通过模拟下游机器学习模型的统计偏好而非人类感知标准，可以成功生成有效的合成证据，进而导致真实漂移现象。

Conclusion: 公开图像扩散模型可被直接用于表格数据的合成攻击，威胁在于攻击者仅需"站在机器角度思考"即可绕过统计审计，而真实漂移机制使得被污染的合成数据在封闭证据流水线中难以被溯源和纠正。

Abstract: Public image diffusion models are now powerful enough that an attacker without the resources to train a tabular-specific generator may repurpose one off the shelf. This study tests that possibility directly. An unmodified Stable Diffusion U-Net is applied to the UCI Adult Income dataset by reshaping each row into a small single-channel pseudo-image. The architecture's inductive bias toward spatial locality makes feature placement a design variable, and several layouts are tested. However, this is only the beginning of the story, as this paper also draws two philosophical distinctions. One separates statistical from perceptual realism: whether synthetic content holds up to a machine's correlation audits or a human's sensory inspection. The other introduces synthetic evidence as a category alongside synthetic media: AI-generated material whose consumer is a machine in a closed evidentiary pipeline rather than a person in an open information system. An attacker succeeds with synthetic evidence by thinking like the machine that will receive it. And the more the attacker succeeds, the more they can induce ground truth drift: the silent reclassification of AI-generated outputs as authentic when reused in pipelines that do not interrogate their provenance.

</details>

### [67] [SHIFT: Robust Double Machine Learning for Average Dose-Response Functions under Heavy-Tailed Contamination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00176)
*Eichi Uehara*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出SHIFT方法，通过结合Welsch损失、渐进非凸优化和基于残差MAD的防御性OLS重拟合，在双机器学习平均剂量响应函数估计中实现对异常值的鲁棒性，并配套极值理论诊断工具，实验表明在污染场景下显著降低RMSE并能高精度识别异常值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双机器学习ADRF估计依赖核加权局部线性平滑器，单个异常值会污染整个核窗口的曲线估计，缺乏鲁棒性。需要设计一种对异常值具有抵抗力的估计方法。

Method: SHIFT包含三个核心组件：(1) 交叉拟合的干扰正交化；(2) 基于核局部Welsch损失的第二阶段估计，通过渐进非凸优化求解；(3) 关键设计：防御性OLS重拟合，其内点截断阈值基于GNC后残差MAD而非原始结果MAD。同时配套六种极值理论诊断方法（Hill、GPD-MLE/PWM、GEV、Mean Excess、参数稳定性、因果尾部系数）帮助区分Frechet/Weibull尾部分布，扩展至二元治疗CATE和时间序列ADRF。

Result: 在p=0.25局部污染压力测试中，level-RMSE从1.03降至0.33；1400次主扫描中最坏情况形状恢复RMSE为0.325（次于Huber-DML的0.276）；在RMSE低于0.35的三个方法中，仅SHIFT产生非均匀样本权重，以平均F1≈0.96（范围0.945-0.968）恢复真实异常值掩码；线性干扰模型（Ridge、Lasso）在均匀污染下优于梯度提升模型，颠覆了通常的"更灵活更好"启发式。

Conclusion: SHIFT通过多种鲁棒技术组合实现了对异常值的有效抵抗，能高精度识别异常值并保持良好曲线恢复性能。配套极值理论诊断工具使实践者可依据经验数据选择SHIFT或L1替代方案。线性干扰模型在鲁棒DML中的优势揭示了干扰模型选择的新见解。

Abstract: Double-machine-learning pipelines for the Average Dose-Response Function rely on kernel-weighted local-linear smoothers, which inherit unbounded functional influence: a single outlier within a kernel window biases the curve across the entire window. We introduce SHIFT (Self-calibrated Heavy-tail Inlier-Fit with Tempering), a robust DML estimator combining cross-fit nuisance orthogonalization with a kernel-local Welsch-loss second stage optimized by Graduated Non-Convexity, and -- the principal design choice -- a defensive OLS refit whose inlier cutoff is scaled by post-GNC residual MAD rather than the raw-outcome MAD. On a localized-contamination stress test at $p=0.25$ this design choice drops level-RMSE from 1.03 to 0.33 while leaving clean and uniformly-contaminated runs unchanged. Across 1,400 main-sweep fits, SHIFT has competitive worst-case shape recovery (RMSE $0.325$ at $p=0.25$, second to Huber-DML's $0.276$); among the three methods with worst-case RMSE below $0.35$, only SHIFT emits a non-uniform per-sample weight vector, recovering the ground-truth outlier mask at mean $F_1 \approx 0.96$ (range $0.945$--$0.968$) on Gaussian-jump DGPs. We pair the estimator with a six-technique Extreme Value Theory diagnostic suite (Hill, GPD-MLE/PWM, GEV, Mean Excess, parameter stability, causal tail coefficient) that lets a practitioner distinguish Frechet from Weibull regimes and choose between SHIFT and L1 alternatives on empirical grounds. Extensions to binary-treatment CATE (Huber pseudo-outcome X-Learner) and time-series ADRF (block-CV + rolling MAD) are included. A counter-intuitive ablation: linear nuisance models (Ridge, Lasso) outperform gradient-boosted nuisances for robust DML under uniform contamination, inverting the usual more-flexible-is-better heuristic.

</details>


### [68] [A unified perspective on fine-tuning and sampling with diffusion and flow models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00229)
*Carles Domingo-Enrich,Yuanqi Du,Michael S. Albergo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了指数倾斜分布下的扩散/流模型采样问题，统一了随机最优控制与非平衡热力学视角，主要贡献包括：（i）偏差-方差分解揭示伴随匹配/采样和新型分数匹配具有有限梯度方差，而目标和条件分数匹配则不然；（ii）伴随ODE的范数界理论支持伴随方法的有效性；（iii）将CMCD和NETS损失函数以及Crooks/Jarzynski恒等式扩展到指数倾斜场景。实验在Stable Diffusion 1.5和3上验证了奖励微调的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指数倾斜分布公式统一了从非归一化密度采样和预训练模型奖励微调两大问题，但现有方法（伴随法、分数匹配、非平衡热力学）缺乏统一的比较框架，且不同方法的梯度方差特性、理论保证尚未明确。

Method: 从随机最优控制视角出发，建立统一框架，通过偏差-方差分解分析不同方法的梯度方差特性，推导伴随ODE的范数界，并将CMCD/NETS损失函数及Crooks/Jarzynski恒等式适配到指数倾斜场景。

Result: 理论上证明了伴随匹配/采样和新型分数匹配具有有限梯度方差而目标和条件分数匹配不具备；建立了伴随方法的理论有效性保证；在Stable Diffusion 1.5和3上验证了所提方法的奖励微调效果。

Conclusion: 指数倾斜框架提供了扩散/流模型采样的统一视角，伴随方法在梯度方差和理论保证上具有优势，扩展的恒等式和损失函数为奖励微调提供了新工具。

Abstract: We study the problem of training diffusion and flow generative models to sample from target distributions defined by an exponential tilting of a base density; a formulation that subsumes both sampling from unnormalized densities and reward fine-tuning of pre-trained models. This problem can be approached from a stochastic optimal control (SOC) perspective, using adjoint-based or score matching methods, or from a non-equilibrium thermodynamics perspective. We provide a unified framework encompassing these approaches and make three main contributions: (i) bias-variance decompositions revealing that Adjoint Matching/Sampling and Novel Score Matching have finite gradient variance, while Target and Conditional Score Matching do not; (ii) norm bounds on the lean adjoint ODE that theoretically support the effectiveness of adjoint-based methods; and (iii) adaptations of the CMCD and NETS loss functions, along with novel Crooks and Jarzynski identities, to the exponential tilting setting. We validate our analysis with reward fine-tuning experiments on Stable Diffusion 1.5 and 3.

</details>


### [69] [Gradient Regularized Newton Boosting Trees with Global Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00581)
*Nikita Zozoulenko,Daniel Falkowski,Thomas Cass,Lukas Gonon*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了受限牛顿下降（Restricted Newton Descent）框架，统一了牛顿提升（Newton boosting）与经典有限维理论。证明了普通牛顿提升在光滑强凸损失下具有线性收敛率，并针对一般凸损失提出了自适应L2正则化牛顿方案，达到O(1/k²)收敛率，数值实验验证了该方案能避免发散问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 牛顿提升（如XGBoost, LightGBM, CatBoost）在表格数据机器学习中表现优异，但其全局收敛性理论尚不明确，远落后于一阶提升方法。现有理论缺乏对牛顿提升在无限维希尔伯特空间中不精确迭代的收敛性分析。

Method: 1) 提出Restricted Newton Descent框架，基于余弦角和弱梯度边概念分析希尔伯特空间上的牛顿法不精确迭代；2) 证明普通牛顿提升在满足Hessian主导条件的光滑强凸损失下具有线性收敛率；3) 针对具有Lipschitz Hessian的一般凸损失，引入自适应ℓ2正则化项（与梯度范数平方根成正比），将梯度正则化牛顿方案扩展到受限弱学习器设置。

Result: 普通牛顿提升在光滑强凸损失下取得线性收敛率；自适应正则化方案在一般凸损失下达到O(1/k²)收敛率，与带Nesterov动量的一阶提升方法速率匹配。数值实验表明，该方案能够收敛而普通牛顿提升可能发散。

Conclusion: 本文为牛顿提升提供了严格的全局收敛性理论，提出的自适应正则化方案在保持二阶方法优势的同时获得了与最优一阶方法相当的收敛速率，为GBDT算法的理论分析和实际改进提供了新方向。

Abstract: Gradient Boosting Decision Trees (GBDTs) dominate tabular machine learning, with modern implementations like XGBoost, LightGBM, and CatBoost being based on Newton boosting: a second-order descent step in the space of decision trees. Despite its empirical success, the global convergence of Newton boosting is poorly understood compared to first-order boosting. In this paper, we introduce Restricted Newton Descent, which studies convex optimization with Newton's method on Hilbert spaces with inexact iterates, based on the concepts of cosine angle and weak gradient edge. Within this framework, we recover Newton boosting with GBDTs and classical finite-dimensional theory as special cases. We first prove that vanilla Newton boosting achieves a linear rate of convergence for smooth, strongly convex losses that satisfy a Hessian-dominance condition. To handle general convex losses with Lipschitz Hessians, we extend a recent gradient regularized Newton scheme to the restricted weak learner setting. This scheme minimally modifies the classical algorithm by introducing an adaptive $\ell_2$-regularization term proportional to the square root of the gradient norm at each iteration. We establish a $\mathcal{O}(\frac{1}{k^2})$ rate for this scheme, thereby obtaining a globally convergent second-order GBDT algorithm with a rate matching that of first-order boosting with Nesterov momentum. In numerical experiments, we show that our scheme converges while vanilla Newton boosting may diverge.

</details>


### [70] [Adaptive Querying with AI Persona Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00696)
*Kaizheng Wang,Yuhang Wu,Assaf Zeevi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于AI人格角色的潜变量模型，将用户状态表示为有限人格词典中的成员，利用大语言模型生成的响应分布作为先验，实现闭式后验更新和高效有限混合预测，从而在严格的问题预算下进行可扩展的贝叶斯自适应查询，用于学习用户依赖的感兴趣量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的贝叶斯设计和计算机化自适应测试通常依赖于限制性的参数假设或昂贵的后验近似，在异质性高维和冷启动场景中应用受限。需要在严格问题预算下高效学习用户依赖的感兴趣量（如对未观测项目的响应和心理测量指标）。

Method: 引入人格诱导潜变量模型（persona-induced latent variable model），用户状态通过有限AI人格词典中的成员关系表示；每种人格对应由大语言模型生成的响应分布；利用该模型获得表达性先验、闭式后验更新和高效有限混合预测，实现可扩展的贝叶斯设计用于顺序项目选择。

Result: 在合成数据和WorldValuesBench上的实验表明，基于人格的后验能够提供准确的概率预测和可解释的自适应启发管道。

Conclusion: 人格诱导潜变量模型能够在异质性高维和冷启动场景下有效进行自适应查询，兼具表达性先验、计算高效性和可解释性。

Abstract: We study adaptive querying for learning user-dependent quantities of interest, such as responses to held-out items and psychometric indicators, within tight question budgets. Classical Bayesian design and computerized adaptive testing typically rely on restrictive parametric assumptions or expensive posterior approximations, limiting their use in heterogeneous, high-dimensional, and cold-start settings. We introduce a persona-induced latent variable model that represents a user's state through membership in a finite dictionary of AI personas, each offering response distributions produced by a large language model. This yields expressive priors with closed-form posterior updates and efficient finite-mixture predictions, enabling scalable Bayesian design for sequential item selection. Experiments on synthetic data and WorldValuesBench demonstrate that persona-based posteriors deliver accurate probabilistic predictions and an interpretable adaptive elicitation pipeline.

</details>

### [71] [Bootstrap Inference under General Two-way Clustering with Serially and Spatially Dependent Common Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00709)
*Ulrich Hounyo,Jiahao Lin*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对双向聚类数据的线性回归模型，开发了自助法推断程序。研究刻画了估计量在五种互斥且完备的渐进行为 regime（三个高斯和两个非高斯），建立了四个不可能性结果，并提出了一种数据驱动的 regime 分类器和基于投影的 wild bootstrap 程序，该程序在四个可行 regime 上实现了一致有效的推断，同时允许第二聚类维度的序列依赖和第一聚类维度的空间依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双向聚类数据在实证研究中普遍存在（如面板数据同时存在个体和时间效应），但现有推断方法在处理这种复杂依赖结构时存在局限性，尤其是在不同渐进 regime 下的统一有效性方面缺乏理论保障。

Method: 首先刻画估计量在五种互斥完备 regime 下的渐进行为；然后建立四个不可能性定理；最后提出数据驱动的 regime 分类器与投影式 wild bootstrap 程序，实现跨可行 regime 的一致有效推断。

Result: 推导了估计量在五个 regime 下的渐近分布；证明了异质性得分成分导致非一致收敛等四个不可能性结果；所提 bootstrap 方法在四个可行 regime 上实现一致有效推断，蒙特卡洛模拟验证了方法的精度和灵活性。

Conclusion: 本文首次在双向聚类文献中实现了 regime 自适应性（regime adaptivity）与灵活依赖结构（沿第二维度的序列依赖和第一维度的空间依赖）的结合，为复杂聚类结构下的推断提供了理论完备且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper develops bootstrap procedures for inference in linear regression models with two-way clustered data. We characterize the estimator's asymptotic behavior in five mutually exclusive and exhaustive regimes: three Gaussian and two non-Gaussian. We establish four impossibility results: heterogeneous score components preclude uniform consistency; uniform consistency also fails in one non-Gaussian (infeasible) regime; the infeasible regime is not uniformly distinguishable from a feasible one; and uniform validity over all feasible regimes rules out uniform conservativeness over the infeasible regime.
  To address the feasible regimes, we propose a data-driven regime classifier and a projection-based wild bootstrap procedure. The procedure delivers uniformly valid inference across the four feasible regimes while allowing serial dependence along the second clustering dimension and spatial dependence along the first. This combination of regime adaptivity and flexible dependence is new to the two-way clustering literature. Monte Carlo simulations confirm the accuracy and flexibility of the proposed methods in settings with complex clustering structures.

</details>

Result: 在保障数据隐私和安全的同时，利用多样化分布式数据集提升了天气建模的准确性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 联邦学习为天气建模提供了一种有效的隐私保护分布式解决方案，能够整合多源传感器数据提升建模性能。

Abstract: Federated weather modeling on sensor data is a distributed system underpinned by federated learning, enabling multiple sensor data sources, including ground weather stations, satellites and IoT devices, to collaboratively train deep learning models without sharing raw data. This method safeguards data privacy and security while leverages diverse, geographically distributed datasets to improve the accuracy and robustness of global/regional weather modeling tasks such as forecasting and anomaly detection.

</details>


### [73] [Borrowed Geometry: Computational Reuse of Frozen Text-Pretrained Transformer Weights Across Modalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00333)
*Abay Bektursun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 冻结的Gemma 4 31B文本预训练权重通过薄可训练接口即可跨模态迁移，在机器人操控、决策Transformer基准和关联记忆任务上超越或匹敌SOTA，且通过双测量协议定位了具体注意力头。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究冻结的纯文本预训练大模型（Gemma 4 31B）能否仅通过极少量可训练参数实现跨模态（如机器人操控、连续控制）的有效迁移，并验证其能力是否源于预训练而非架构本身。

Method: 使用冻结的Gemma 4 31B权重（仅文本预训练），通过薄可训练接口（线性层）适配不同下游任务；采用双测量协议（文本激活探针+非语言任务消融）定位关键注意力头；通过随机权重对照、从头训练对照等实验进行架构假说验证。

Result: (1) OGBench场景任务SOTA (+4.33pt)；(2) D4RL Walker2d达到DT同等性能且参数量仅0.43×；(3) 关联记忆任务上冻结切片+113K线性接口实现8.7×优势；(4) 随机权重和随机初始化均失败，验证预训练必要性；(5) 定位到特定注意力头（如L26.28）在两种协议中均被识别为关键。

Conclusion: 冻结的文本预训练大模型可通过极薄接口跨模态迁移并达到SOTA，其能力来源于预训练权重而非架构；成功定位了具体注意力头作为跨模态能力的关键载体；但跨模型复现受限于Gemma 4 31B作为2026年4月小规模Pareto前沿的唯一模型。

Abstract: Frozen Gemma 4 31B weights pretrained exclusively on text tokens, unmodified, transfer across modality boundaries through a thin trainable interface. (1) OGBench scene-play-singletask-task1-v0: $+4.33$pt over published GCIQL at $n=3$ with std 0.74 -- a published-SOTA win on a robotic manipulation task the substrate has never seen. (2) D4RL Walker2d-medium-v2: Decision-Transformer parity ($76.2 \pm 0.8$, $n=3$) at $0.43\times$ DT's trainable count, with the frozen substrate compressing to a 5L slice ($+1.66$pt over the 6L baseline at $n=3$). (3) Associative recall as the cleanest pretraining-load-bearing case: the frozen slice + a 113K-parameter linear interface reaches L30 best-checkpoint per-bit error 0.0505 ($n=2$); a 6.36M-parameter from-scratch trained transformer at matched capacity ($1/\sqrt{d_k}$ scaling, two seeds, LR sweep) cannot solve the task at all under the protocol (best L30 = 0.4395), an $8.7\times$ advantage. Architecture-alone falsifications: a frozen random transformer with correct $1/\sqrt{d_k}$ scaling stays at random-chance loss for 50k steps; a random-init Gemma slice fails OGBench cube-double-play-task1 entirely (0.89% across $n=3$ where pretrained reaches 60%). A dual-measurement protocol -- text-activation probing on 95 English sentences plus task-ablation on a non-language target -- names individual heads independently identifiable on both protocols: head L26.28 scores $3.7\times$ the slice mean for English token-copying and is the #2 most-critical head for binary copy ablation ($Δ$ L30 $= +0.221$); three further heads (L27.28, L27.2, L27.3) classify by the same protocol. The mechanism is single-model and the cross-modality results are single-task within their respective benchmarks; cross-model replication is structurally constrained because Gemma 4 31B is the only model on the small-scale Pareto frontier as of April 2026.

</details>


### [74] [Free Energy Surface Sampling via Reduced Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00337)
*Zichen Liu,Tiejun Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FES-FM方法，利用简化流匹配技术在集体变量空间中直接采样自由能表面，大幅降低计算成本并提高采样精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自由能表面采样是统计物理中的关键问题，传统方法需要在高维构型空间中模拟并投影到CV空间，计算成本高昂。作者旨在开发一种能直接在CV空间采样的高效方法，降低计算开销。

Method: 基于简化流匹配（FM）方法，在集体变量（CV）空间中训练动力学传输映射，实现自由能表面的直接采样。针对多粒子系统，利用势能局部极小值处的Hessian矩阵构建先验分布，确保旋转-平移不变性和物理合理性。

Result: 在多种势函数和集体变量上进行的对比实验表明，该方法在单位采样时间内大幅降低了计算成本，同时具有更优越的精度。

Conclusion: FES-FM通过流匹配在CV空间直接采样自由能表面，显著提升了采样效率与精度，为统计物理中的自由能计算提供了一种高效替代方案。

Abstract: Sampling the free energy surface, namely, the distribution of collective variables (CVs), is a crucial problem in statistical physics, as it underpins a better understanding of chemical reactions and conformational transitions. Traditional methods for free energy surface sampling involve simulation in high-dimensional configuration space and projecting the resulting configurations onto the CV space. To reduce the computational costs of such sampling, we propose FES-FM, a reduced flow matching (FM) method for free energy sampling (FES). We train a dynamical transport map in the CV space, thereby enabling direct sampling of the free energy surface. For many-particle systems, we construct a prior distribution based on the Hessian at a local minimum of the potential, which ensures both rotation-translation invariance and physically meaningful configurations. We evaluate the proposed method across a variety of potential functions and collective variables.
